# PDO rewrite — Stage 0 inventory and staged plan

Branch: `feature/pdo-rewrite-20261108` (isolated worktree; shared `C:/Projects/Quire` remains untouched).

## Scope and counts

Inventory uses Roslyn C# syntax trees for files under `src/` and `tests/`, excluding `obj/` and `bin/`. Type references are recorded from C# type positions, not ordinary identifiers with coincidental names. Namespace imports with no matching declared type use are reported separately. Generated means `Generated/` or `.g.cs`.

- Persistent-data source files: **149** (111 under `PersistentDataObjects`, 38 under `PersistentData`).
- Declared types: **154 distinct names**; **155 declaration nodes** including nested declarations.
- C# source/test files: **7,346** scanned.
- Files with a declared persistence type reference: **435**; type-reference occurrences: **3191**.
- Namespace-import-only files: **12**.
- Mirrored member/type dependencies: **886** source locations across **193 original mirrored type/member identities**.

## Persistent-data type/member inventory

Every source declaration file, type, and member is listed. Member descriptions are the declaration signatures; enum values are also enumerated.

## Proposed staged order

1. **Stage 0 (this commit):** inventory and plan only; no deletions or code changes.
2. **Stage 1 — non-mirrored consumers:** rewrite application and test code onto established non-PersistentDataObjects APIs, one owning project at a time. Keep DTO declarations and mirrored members unchanged.
3. **Stage 2 — mirrored consumers:** assess each listed member separately. Rewrite only if Release IL remains identical; D21 permits changes only to members with no Mathcad counterpart both before and after. Do not change a mirrored signature or body where the gate requires its original counterpart.
4. **Stage 3 — generated reference cleanup:** regenerate generated mirrors after handwritten consumers no longer need these types. Never edit `Generated/` manually; generated member signatures remain gate-sensitive.
5. **Stage 4 — remove unreferenced persistence types:** delete only declarations with no remaining references after earlier stages. Full removal of both families is blocked while mirrored signatures or bodies still require their original metadata.

## Gate and mirror constraints

- A mirrored signature that references a persistence type cannot change without changing metadata and failing Release IL parity. A body reference is also protected when the member has a Mathcad counterpart or is selected by the gate.
- The inventory shows 442 mirrored signature references, 374 body references, and 70 type-level references. Members with Mathcad counterparts remain protected by Release IL parity; D21 can permit only members without a counterpart both before and after.
- **Full deletion (Stage 4) is blocked by current mirror rules:** 193 original mirrored types/member identities still reference this family. Their matching metadata requires the persistence types, so retain compatibility declarations unless the maintainer changes the target architecture or gate.
- Generated code is rewritten by MirrorGen and must not be hand-edited.
- Stage 0 changes no source or generated files; no persistence declarations are deleted.
